# The gap ledger: blocks of the original that no run can reach, which coverage.py --gaps counts
# as explained. One block per line, by its function and its offset in it, with what keeps runs
# out and the evidence for it:
#
#     FUNCTION+OFFSET KIND # evidence: the analysis, or the callers and the values they pass
#
# KIND is dead (no value that reaches the branch takes it), dev (developer-only code that nothing
# in the game turns on), fail (code that runs only once the game has failed: exception handlers,
# crash dumps, exit), hw (code that only hardware the SDK layer leaves out runs, or that the SDK
# layer stands in for) or oracle (provisional: code every game runs that lockstep cannot check,
# because it never returns or runs as an exception handler; the replay rotation's frame
# comparison with Dolphin covers what it does). A block that odd state can reach, such as a null
# check the game never trips, belongs to probes and mutated checks instead, not here.
#
# A function whose name other units also use is named UNIT:FUNCTION (its unit as the decomp's
# asm tree names it, such as dolphin/axfx/reverb_hi:HandleReverb).
#
# FUNCTION+* covers every block of a function that nothing verified, for the functions no run can
# reach that are not verified by probes, and for those whose every block the evidence covers,
# such as the crash screen's.
DSPCheckMailToDSP+* hw # the SDK layer stands in for it (ssbm-run STAND_INS): neither the original nor the port runs
DSPCheckMailFromDSP+* hw # the SDK layer stands in for it (ssbm-run STAND_INS): neither the original nor the port runs
DSPReadMailFromDSP+* hw # the SDK layer stands in for it (ssbm-run STAND_INS): neither the original nor the port runs

FIOQuery+* dev # dolphin/mcc/fio: host file I/O over a dev kit's USB adapter
hsd_80394314+* dev # debugconsole_main: the HSD debug console, which nothing in the retail game opens
fn_80397814+* dev # debugconsole_main: the HSD debug console, which nothing in the retail game opens
printf+* dead # its one caller, gmtoulib at 8018F4D8, prints "impossible stage num" for a stage mnSelStageRandom does not return
OSDefaultExceptionHandler+* fail # the OS's handler for exceptions nothing else takes: a failing game
__OSUnhandledException+* fail # reports an exception no handler takes and halts
OSDumpContext+* fail # prints the registers of a failed context, for __OSUnhandledException
DMAErrorHandler+* fail # reports a failed DMA transfer
PPCHalt+* fail # halts the CPU for good, after a failure or exit
exit+* fail # MSL exit: the game never exits; only a failure path would reach it
_ExitProcess+* fail # the EABI exit hook, after exit
__assert+* fail # never returns; coverage.py already leaves out blocks that call it
__OSDispatchInterrupt+* hw # the exception vector's interrupt dispatcher: the SDK layer reads __OSInterruptTable and calls the handler itself (crates/sdk/src/lib.rs, deliver_one)
ExternalInterruptHandler+* hw # the external interrupt exception vector, which the SDK layer stands in for as above
OSLoadContext+* hw # switches threads by returning from an exception (rfi), which the interpreter does not emulate; thread switches are the SDK layer's
OSSwitchFPUContext+* hw # the floating-point unavailable exception's handler; the interpreter raises no exceptions
GXCPInterruptHandler+* hw # command processor FIFO overflow/underflow interrupt, which the SDK layer never raises
GXTokenInterruptHandler+* hw # pixel engine token interrupt, which the SDK layer never raises
__OSResetSWInterruptHandler+* hw # the console's reset switch, which no run presses
__ARChecksize+* hw # sizes ARAM by probing the hardware at boot; the SDK layer sets ARAM up instead
__fstLoad+* hw # loads the disc's file table for the apploader path the SDK layer's boot replaces
__start+* oracle # the boot entry: never returns, so no lockstep check can compare it; every run starts here
main+* oracle # never returns: it runs gm_801A4510, the scene loop, for good
gm_801A4510+* oracle # the scene loop, which never returns
gm_801A4D34+* oracle # runs a scene's frames until it ends, inside the scene loop: one call spans a whole scene of interrupts and video fields, which a check cannot replay

# MCC and FIO, the dev kit's USB link to a host (2026-10-02): MCCInit runs only from hsd_803931A4, which main calls when DbLevel is not Master and R is held at boot, and from usb_exit_init on an event only MCCInit's callback queues. A retail disc runs at Master (no /develop.ini), so MCC is never initialized and every MCC and FIO call takes its not-initialized exit, which probes verified; the rest needs the link and a USB adapter on EXI, which the SDK layer leaves out.

# HIO, the host I/O under MCC (2026-10-02): callers.py lists only MCC functions calling into dolphin/hio (HIOInit and HIOEnumDevices from MCCInit and MCCEnumDevices, the reads and writes from MCC's channel and mailbox code). Every HIO call past its first test needs Chan, which only HIOInit sets after an EXI device answers id 0x01010000 (the dev kit's USB adapter), or EXIGetID returning that id; ExiHandler, DbgHandler, TxHandler and RxHandler run a callback only HIOInit installs on that success. The SDK layer models no such device, so HIOInit always takes its no-device exit, and mutated checks cannot pass the EXI calls, whose stand-ins return 0 for them.
